              聚 焦大灣區
              The Company intensifies its development in
              Shenzhen and focuses on the Greater Bay Area

      Shenzhen Investment Limited has been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
      Limited since 1997. Shum Yip Group, being its controlling shareholder, is an integrated conglomerate
      directly managed by State-owned Assets Supervision and Administration Commission of Shenzhen Municipal.
      Positioned as an “Innovative Constructor of Industrial Cities, Wealthy Livelihood Operator”, the Company’s core
      businesses include property development, property investment and comprehensive urban operation services, and
      it also engages in innovative businesses such as high-tech agriculture and high-end manufacturing, endeavoring to
      transform and upgrade into a technology-based industry group focusing on the development of urban complex and
      investment in technology industry.

      By intensifying its development in Shenzhen, focusing on the Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area and
      planning for its development in the Yangtze River Delta and high-tier cities such as key provincial capital cities, the Group
      currently has a land reserve with gross floor area of approximately 6.74 million square meters in various cities of China,
      of which the quality land reserve with gross floor area of approximately 4.44 million square meters is located in the
      Greater Bay Area. The Company is committed to optimizing its land reserves structure, and concentrates its assets and                          Shum Yip Natural City
                                                                                                                                                     深業山水東城花園
      business in the Greater Bay Area as well as high-tier cities in key regions.

      It has all along been the Company’s core strategic goal to promote the long term sustainable development of its
      business, with an aim to bring a stable return and create value appreciation for its shareholders.

      深圳控股有限公司於1997年在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，其控股股東深業集團是深圳市國
      資委直管的大型綜合性企業集團。公司按照「產城創新建設者、幸福民生運營商」的定位，
      以物業開發、物業投資及城市綜合運營服務為核心業務，同時涉足高科農
      業、高端製造等創新業務，致力於轉型升級成以城市綜合開發運營、科技
      產業的投資服務為主業的科技型產業集團。

      公司深耕深圳，聚焦粵港澳大灣區，佈局長三角區域及重點省會等高能
      級城市，目前本集團於國內多個城市合共擁有儲備建築面積約674萬平
      方米的土地儲備，其中位於大灣區的優質土地儲備的建築面積約444
      萬平方米。公司致力優化土地儲備的結構，將資產和業務向大灣區
      和重點區域的高能級城市集中。

      公司一貫之核心策略目標，乃致力推動業務長期持續發展，
      以期為股東帶來整體穩定回報及締造價值增長。

                                                                                                        Chairman’s Statement
                                                                                                                               主席報告




“Promote stability and
development with progress,
and seek transformation through
breakthroughs after building-up”

「以進促穩保發展，先立後破謀轉型」


                                                                                                                       Shum Yip Zhongshan
                                                                                                                       Future Gate
                                                                                                                       深業中山未來之門


In 2023, the unfavorable factors in the international political and economic            2023 年，國際政治經濟環境不利因素增多，美元利
environment increased, while the US dollar interest rate continued to rise,             率不斷攀升，國內經濟發展形勢錯綜複雜，周期性和
the domestic economic development situation was complex, cyclical and                   結構性矛盾疊加，房地產供求關係發生重大變化，房
structural contradictions were superimposed, and the real estate supply and             地產市場處於下行尋底、築底階段。面對嚴峻的市場
demand underwent significant changes. The real estate market was in a                   環境，集團堅持穩健經營主基調，將穩增長和高質量
downward bottoming out and bottom-out stage. In the face of the severe market           發展相結合，積極迎難而上，推進各項經營工作。儘
environment, the Group adhered to the main principle of stable operation,               管2023年合同銷售逆市增長突破新高，但由於所銷售
combined steady growth with high-quality development, and proactively rose to           項目大部分未達結轉條件，現貨去化不及預期，本集
the challenge to push forward its various operational efforts. Although contracted      團收入結轉規模有較明顯下滑，加上聯營公司虧損、
sales in 2023 broke through to a new high against the market growth, the                財務成本增加等因素的疊加，本公司權益股東應佔虧
Group’s revenue carryover scale declined more significantly due to the fact             損2.6 億港元；如剔除本集團所屬投資物業和金融資
that most of the projects sold did not meet the carryover conditions and the            產公允價值變動的淨影響及聯合營公司業績影響，本
sales of inventories were less than expected. Coupled with the combination of           公司權益股東應佔溢利19.2億港元。董事會建議本公
factors such as losses from associates and an increase in finance costs, the            司以現金方式向股東派發2023年末期股息每股7.00港
Company suffered a loss attributable to equity shareholders of HK$261 million.          仙，以回饋股東。
Taking no account of the net effect of changes in the fair value of the Group’s
investment properties and financial assets owned by the Group, as well as the
effect of results of associates and joint ventures, the profit attributable to equity
shareholders of the Company would have been HK$1.92 billion. The Board of
Directors recommends that the Company pay a final dividend of HK7.00 cents per
share for 2023 in cash as a return to shareholders.

 Chairman’s Statement
 主席報告




 2023 Business Review                                                                2023年經營情況回顧
 Focus on the principal business, with steady development of                         聚焦主業，產城業務穩健發展
 industrial-city operations
 Contracted sales grew against the market trend                                      合同銷售逆市增長
 Against the backdrop of an overall downturn in the real estate market, we           在房地產市場整體下行的背景下，2023 全年實現地
 achieved contracted real estate sales of approximately RMB26.6 billion in 2023,     產合同銷售約266億元人民幣，同比增長38%，超額
 a year-on-year increase of 38%, exceeding the annual target. Upper Coast, with      完成年度目標。雲海灣項目單盤銷售額133億元人民
 a sales amount of RMB13.3 billion for a single property, became the double          幣，成為2023 年全國推售套數最多、成交金額最大

 Overall Results                                                                        整體業績
 Since 2023, the relationship between supply and demand in the real estate              2023 年以來，房地產行業供求關係發生重大變化，
 industry has changed significantly. The central government has set the tone            中央定調「穩支柱、防風險、促需求」，各地陸續出

 launched, but the market recovery was still restricted by factors such as the          續性也顯不足。面對嚴峻低迷的行業環境，本集團堅
 continued downward expectation of the industry, and the sustainability of the          持「穩健經營」的主基調，努力克服行業下行等不利

 the industry, the Group adhered to the principle of “steady operation”, strived        動各項工作進展。
 to overcome the adverse impact of the industry downturn and other obstacles,

revenue and profitability. The Group has established corresponding management         管理及決策機制，未來將繼續優化內控體系，同時密
and decision-making mechanisms for investment business, and will continue to          切監控市場動態，積極應對外部環境帶來的風險。
optimize the internal control system in the future, while closely monitoring market
dynamics and actively responding to risks from the external environment.

 controlling Shareholder (the “Loan Renewal Agreement”) pursuant to which            司同意就貸款展期，並將貸款之償還日期延期至2024
 the Subsidiary agreed to renew the loan and extend the repayment date of the        年 3 月 12 日。由於非控股股東為附屬公司的主要股
 loan to 12 March 2024. As the Non-controlling Shareholder is a substantial          東，其為本公司在附屬公司層面的關連人士。因此，
 shareholder of the Subsidiary, it is a connected person of the Company at           貸款展期協議項下擬進行之交易構成本公司的關連交
 the subsidiary level, the transaction contemplated under the Loan Renewal           易，並須遵守上市規則第14A章的公告及申報規定，

 The Company is committed to maintaining high standard of corporate governance.          本公司致力恪守奉行高標準之企業管治。董事會（「董
 The board of Directors (the “Board”) strongly believes that good corporate              事會」）堅信，良好企業管治對有效之管理、健全之

 successful business growth and enhancing shareholders’ value. The Company               重要。本公司已於整個2023年應用並遵守香港聯合交
 has applied the principles and complied with all code provisions of the Corporate       易所有限公司證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄C1所

 Remuneration Committee                                                               薪酬委員會
 The Remuneration Committee comprises three members, all being Independent            薪 酬 委 員 會 由 三 名 成 員（ 均 為 獨 立 非 執 行 董 事 ）組
 Non-executive Directors. It is chaired by Dr. WONG Yau Kar, David and other          成。薪酬委員會由黃友嘉博士擔任主席，其他成員為
 members are Mr. LI Wai Keung and Prof. GONG Peng.                                    李偉強先生及宮鵬教授。

 The Company adopted the model whereby the Remuneration Committee is                  本公司已採納由薪酬委員會負責根據其職權範圍就個
 responsible for making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration               別執行董事及高級管理人員的薪酬待遇向董事會提出
 packages of individual Executive Director and senior management according to its     建議的模式。薪酬委員會亦負責就非執行董事的薪酬
 terms of reference. The Remuneration Committee is also responsible for making        事項向董事會提出建議、設立恰當及透明的程序以制
 recommendation to the Board on the remuneration matters of Non-executive             訂薪酬、根據上市規則第17章審閱及 ╱ 或批准購股權
 Directors, the setting up of adequate and transparent procedures for setting         計劃相關事項。薪酬委員會的職權範圍全文可於本公
 such remuneration, reviewing and/or approving matters relating to share option       司網站及聯交所網站內查閱。
 schemes under Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules. The full terms of reference of the
 Remuneration Committee are available on the Company’s website and the Stock
 Exchange’s website.

 The Group’s Remuneration Policy aims to ensure that the remuneration level is        本集團的薪酬政策旨在確保薪酬水平適當並與本公司
 appropriate and align with the Company’s goals, objectives and performance,          的目標、宗旨及表現保持一致，以便招聘及挽留優秀
 so as to facilitate the recruitment and retention of outstanding directors, senior   董事、高級管理人員及員工，並為彼等提供充足動力
 management and employees and to provide them with adequate incentive                 以達成業務目標。於釐定董事及僱員薪酬時，須考
 for achieving business targets. When determining Directors’ and employees’           慮包括整體市場及房地產行業薪酬水平；相關職位及
 remuneration, factors including salary levels in the market as a whole and in        工作領域的市場基準；本集團及個人的表現在內的因
 the real estate industry; market benchmarks for relevant positions and work          素。亦將根據個人表現及本集團業績向僱員提供花紅
 areas; the performance of the Group and the individual etc. shall be considered.     及其他獎勵（包括購股權）。此外，薪酬方案每年進
 Bonuses and other incentives including share options will also be provided to        行一次審閱，以確保內部公平及競爭力。
 employees based on individual performance and the results of the Group. And,
 remuneration packages are reviewed on an annual basis to ensure internal equity
 and competitiveness.

 No Director or any of his/her associates is allowed to determine their own           任何董事或其任何聯繫人士不得自行釐訂其本身的薪
 remuneration. In making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration of           酬。在向董事會建議董事及高級管理人員的薪酬時，
 Directors and senior management, the Remuneration Committee will take into           薪酬委員會將考慮本集團薪酬政策所載的因素。
 account the factors set out in the Group’s Remuneration Policy.

 During the year, the Remuneration Committee reviewed the remuneration of             年內，薪酬委員會已審議執行董事及高級管理層之薪
 Executive Directors and senior management. The Remuneration Committee also           酬。薪酬委員會亦檢討了本集團的整體薪酬架構，並
 reviewed the overall remuneration structure of the Group and considered the          審議了各種以表現為基準的激勵安排（包括購股權）的
 forms and adequacy of the various performance-based incentive arrangements           形式及充足性。
 including share options.

 The Board Nomination Policy sets out the principles which guide the Nomination      董事會提名政策載有指引提名委員會物色及評估候選
 Committee to identify and evaluate a candidate for nomination to (i) the Board      人的原則以(i)就委任向董事會或(ii)就選舉向本公司股
 for appointment or (ii) the shareholders of the Company for election, as a          東提名為董事，旨在確保董事會具備適用於本公司業

Risk Management and Internal Control                                                風險管理與內部監控
Responsibility                                                                      責任
The Board is responsible for the risk management and internal control systems       董事會須對風險管理及內部監控系統負責，有責任檢
and reviewing the effectiveness of such systems. Management is responsible for      討該等制度的有效性，而管理層則負責設計及執行內
designing and implementing an internal control system to manage the risks to the    部監控制度以最大限度的管理公司面臨的風險，達
Company to the maximum extent, in order to identify and manage these risks so       到識別和管理這些風險，從而可以掌握、降低、舒
as to understand, reduce, mitigate, divert or avoid these risks. The Board hereby   緩、轉移或規避這些風險。董事會謹在此闡釋該等系
explains that the systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate, the risk   統旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且
of failure to meet business objectives, and only to provide reasonable, but not     只能就不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕
absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss.                          對的保證。

       established and maintained by the Company.                                       險管理架構。

       the risk management framework.                                                   監察方面的工作。

 Risk Management Process                                                                 風險管理程序
 The Company adopts an integrated “bottom-up” and “top-down” risk review                 公司採用「由下而上」的方式配合「由上而下」的綜合
 process, requiring the heads of functional departments and business units of the        風險檢討程序，要求公司職能部門及業務部門主管參
 Company to participate in identifying operational risks and incorporate them into       與識別營運風險，納入各項業務及決策流程中，包括
 various business and decision-making processes, including strategy formulation,         策略制定、業務發展、業務規劃、資金分配、投資
 business development, business planning, capital allocation, investment                 決定、內部監控及日常營運等，以便於
 decisions, internal controls and daily operations, to enable

 (1)   comprehensive identification and prioritization of all significant risks within   (1)   全面識別公司內部所有的重大風險，並進行優
       the Company,                                                                            次排序，

 (2)   reporting of significant risks to the appropriate management level,               (2)   將重大風險上報至適當的管理層級別，

 (3)   the management to effectively communicate about the risks.                        (3)   讓管理層就風險進行有效溝通。

•   Business units and functional departments of the Company conduct risk         •   業務部門和公司職能部門對職責範圍內的具
    identification and evaluation of specific businesses within the scope of          體業務進行風險辨識、評估，提出和實施風

    significant risks identified during the risk management process.

    own business processes, collecting and collating internal and external            持續地收集、整理本部門職能範圍內的內
    risk initial information within their scope of functions extensively and          部、外部風險初始信息。風險管理部定期
    continuously. The risk management department regularly screens,                   對各部門收集的風險初始信息進行必要的篩
    refines, compares, classifies and combines the initial risk information           選、提煉、對比、分類、組合，並根據各
    collected by each department as necessary and draws the risk                      部門的風險評估結果，繪製風險坐標圖，初
    coordinates map based on the risk evaluation results of each department           步確定公司本部各項風險的管理優先順序、
    to initially determine the management priorities of various risks of the          制定風險管理策略和提出和實施風險管理解
    Company, formulate risk management strategies, and propose and                    決方案。
    implement risk management solutions.

    screening and sequencing and interview procedures, compiles the                   驟以及訪談程序，編寫企業全面風險管理年

    Report on Internal Control and Risk Management Systems”.                          估報告》。

    Control and Risk Management Systems” to the Audit Committee and the               報告》供審核委員會及董事會審閱。
    Board for their review.

    Company, and reviews the effectiveness of the risk management and                 險，檢討公司風險管理及內部監控系統的成
    internal control systems of the Company on an annual basis.                       效。

•   In addition to the risk evaluation function, the management from the          •   有關風險評估職能外，職能部門管理層還分
    functional departments is responsible for identifying and evaluating the          別從投資審核及戰略規劃、財務管理、法律

    management and legal compliance respectively, and forms a normalized              制，定期開展系統性重大風險評估工作，實
    mechanism for risk evaluation to regularly conduct systematic significant         現了重大風險動態管理。
    risk evaluation, so as to achieve dynamic management of significant
    risks.

    out systematic significant risk evaluation, identify, evaluate and analyze        性重大風險評估工作，對當前經營發展中存
    all kinds of existing or potential risks in current business development,         在或潛在的各類風險進行識別、評估和分

    management of significant risks.

    for approval so that significant business risks are effectively controlled.       有效地控制。

Internal Auditing                                                                        內部審計
The risk management department is responsible for the internal audit of the              風險管理部門負責公司內部審計，運用其設立的《全
Company. It reviews the risks involved in daily operations of the Company                面風險管理手冊》及《內部審計工作管理暫行辦法》中
and its subsidiaries by using the audit plans and procedures set out in the              清晰列明的審核計劃及程序，檢討公司及附屬企業的
“Comprehensive Risk Management Manual” and the “Interim Measures for                     日常運營面臨的風險，並對內部審計項目做定期後評
Internal Audit Management” it established, and conducts regular post-evaluation          價檢討以保證風險管理架構的成效。
reviews on internal audit items to ensure the effectiveness of risk management
framework.

Each year, the risk management department determines the internal audit work             風險管理部每年度按照公司戰略部署，根據經營管理
priorities and formulates annual internal audit plans according to the Company’s         需要和公司的年度工作安排，確定內部審計工作重
strategic deployment, business management needs and annual work schedule.                點，制定年度內部審計計劃。

The annual internal audit plan shall include the following basic elements:               年度內部審計計劃當包括以下基本內容：

1)   Annual work objectives of internal audit;                                           1)    內部審計年度工作目標；

2)   The specific audit items to be implemented and their sequence;                      2)    需要執行的具體審計項目及其先後順序；

3)   The audit resources allocated to each audit item.                                   3)    各審計項目所分配的審計資源。

According to the “Interim Measures for Internal Audit Management”, matters that          按照《內部審計工作管理暫行辦法》，必須進行審計的
must be audited shall include the following:                                             事項包括下列各項：


 Type of Audit                                    Content
 審計類型                                             內容
 Economic Responsibility Audit                    •      Expiration of the term of office of the principal leaders of the enterprise;
 經濟責任審計                                           •      企業主要領導人任期屆滿的；

                                                  •      Due to redesignation, removal, dismissal, retirement, etc., during the term of office;
                                                  •      任期內因調任、免職、辭退、退休等；

                                                  •      Assets reorganization of the enterprise such as transfer of control, mixed ownership
                                                         reform and change of ownership management level;
                                                  •      企業進行控制權轉讓、混合所有制改革、產權管理層級變更等資產重組的；

                                                  •      Bankruptcy or dissolution of the enterprise;
                                                  •      企業破產的、解散的；

                                                  •      Others that are subject to economic responsibility audit.
                                                  •      其他需要進行經濟責任審計的。

 Special Audit                                    At least one special audit every three years on the subsidiaries is carried out to achieve
                                                  full coverage of audit supervision.

 專項審計                                             對所屬企業每三年內至少開展一次專項審計，實現審計監督全覆蓋。

 Post-Evaluation Audit                            After an investment project is completed, a comprehensive post-evaluation audit of its
                                                  operation and economic benefits is required.

 後評價審計                                            投資項目全部完成後，需要對其運作和經濟效益等進行全面的後評價審計。

materially misstated.

Responsibilities of Directors and Audit Committee for the                             董事及審核委員會就綜合財務報表須承擔的責任
Consolidated Financial Statements
The directors of the Company are responsible for the preparation of the               貴公司董事須負責根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港
consolidated financial statements that give a true and fair view in accordance        財務報告準則》及香港《公司條例》擬備真實而中肯的
with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA and the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance,               綜合財務報表，並對其認為為使綜合財務報表的擬備
and for such internal control as the directors determine is necessary to enable       不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述所需的
the preparation of consolidated financial statements that are free from material      內部控制負責。
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are responsible     在擬備綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估 貴集團持續
for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as      經營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與持續經營有關的
applicable, matters related to going concern and using the going concern basis        事項，以及使用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事有意
of accounting unless the directors either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease   將 貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他實際的替代
operations, or have no realistic alternative but to do so.                            方案。

The Audit Committee are responsible for overseeing the Group’s financial              審核委員會須負責監督 貴集團的財務報告過程。
reporting process.

 Financial Statements
 Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated      我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由於

 of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a       理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照《香港審計
 guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always detect         準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能
 a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or         發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預
 error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could    期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者

 3     Financial risk management                                                          3   財務風險管理
       The Group’s major operations are mainly located in the PRC and is                      本集團之主要業務大部份位於中國，受特定因
       subject to special considerations and significant risks. These include                 素影響，承受重大風險。該等風險包括（其中包
       risks associated with, among others, the political, economic and legal                 括）政治、經濟及法律環境、政府當局對價格管
       environment, influence of national authorities over pricing regulation and             控的影響以及市場競爭。
       competition in the market.

          are provided respectively.                                                              提撥備。

              significantly or whether the assets have been impaired, the Group                 已減值而定，本集團按不同資產的信貸風

              different assets.

         The definition of credit-impaired assets                                              信貸減值資產的定義

         occurs, the defined standards adopted by the Group are consistent                     否出現信貸減值，本集團於考慮定性及定

         financial assets, while considering quantitative and qualitative                      內部信貸風險管理目標一致。本集團評估
         indicators. When the Group assesses whether the debtor has credit                     債務人是否出現信貸減值時，主要考慮下
         impairment, the following factors are mainly considered:                              列因素：

          •    The debt has overdue for more than 90 days after the contract                        •     債務於合約付款日期後欠款逾期超過
               payment date.                                                                              90日。

               contractual reasons due to the debtor’s financial difficulties,                            經濟或合約理由向債務人授出貸款人

               the lender.

          •    The debtor has significant financial difficulties.                                   •     債務人遇上重大財務困難。

          •    The debtor is likely to go bankrupt or needs other financial                         •     債務人很可能破產或需要其他財務重
               restructuring.                                                                             組。

3.2 Capital management                                                                 3.2 資本管理
    The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s              本集團管理資本目標為維持本集團持續經營能
    ability to continue as a going concern, to provide returns for shareholders            力，以為股東提供回報為其他利益相關方創造
    and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital                 利益，並保持最佳資本結構以減低資金成本。
    structure to reduce the cost of capital.

     In order to optimise the capital structure, the Group may adjust the amount               為優化資本架構，本集團可能會調整已向股東
     of dividends paid to shareholders, repurchase of shares, issue new shares                 支付的股息金額、回購股份、發行新股或出售
     or sell assets.                                                                           資產。

 3.3 Fair value estimation                                                              3.3 公允值估計
     The table below analyses financial instruments carried or presented at fair            下表按計量公允值所用估值方法之輸入值層級分

     value. The different levels are defined as follows:                                    界定如下：

       •       Quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets            •   同等資產或負債之活躍市場報價（未經調
               or liabilities. The quoted prices already incorporates the market’s              整）。所報價格已納入市場對經濟環境變
               assumptions with respect to changes in economic climate such                     化（如利率上升及通脹）以及因環境、社
               as rising interest rates and inflation, as well as changes due to                會治理（「ESG」）風險所導致變化的假設
               Environmental, Social and Governance (“ESG”) risks (level 1)                     （第一級）

       •       Inputs other than quoted prices included within level 1 that are             •   除第一級所包括報價外，資產或負債之可
               observable for the assets or liabilities, either directly (that is, as           觀察輸入值，可為直接（即例如價格）或
               prices) or indirectly (that is, derived from prices) (level 2)                   間接（即源自價格）
                                                                                                        （第二級）

       •       Inputs for the assets or liabilities that are not based on observable        •   就非上市股權證券及ESG風險會導致重大

               unlisted equity securities and for instruments where ESG risk gives              之輸入值並非基於可觀察市場數據（即不

    Segment performance is evaluated based on reportable segment profit,                     分類表現乃按可呈報分類溢利進行評估，可呈

    operations. The adjusted profit before taxation is measured consistently                 利。經調整除稅前溢利乃按與本集團之除稅
    with the Group’s profit before taxation except that other income and                     前溢利一致之方式計量，惟其他收入及其他利
    other gains, finance costs, net fair value gain or loss on financial assets              得、融資成本、透過損益按公允值計算之金融
    measured at fair value through profit or loss as well as head office and                 資產公允值收益或虧損淨額，以及總辦事處及
    corporate expenses are excluded from such measurement.                                   公司開支並不包括在此計量內。

(b) Accounting policies of revenue recognition                                           (b) 收益確認之會計政策
    Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it arises from the sale of             當本集團於日常業務過程中提供服務或他人根據
    goods, the provision of services or the use by others of the Group’s assets              租賃使用本集團資產而產生銷售貨品收入時，
    under leases in the ordinary course of the Group’s business.                             本集團將收入分類為收入。

     Revenue is recognised when control over a product or service is transferred               當產品或服務之控制權轉移至客戶或承租人有
     to the customer, or the lessee has the right to use the asset, at the amount              權使用資產時，按本集團預期將享有的承諾代
     of promised consideration to which the Group is expected to be entitled,                  價金額確認收入，惟不包括代第三方收取的款
     excluding those amounts collected on behalf of third parties. Revenue                     項。收入不包括增值稅或其他銷售稅，並經扣
     excludes value added tax or other sales taxes and is after deduction of any               減任何貿易折扣後計算。
     trade discounts.

     Where the contract contains a financing component which provides a                        倘合約載有向客戶提供超過12 個月的重大融資
     significant financing benefit to the customer for more than 12 months,                    利益的融資組成部份，則收入按應收金額的現
     revenue is measured at the present value of the amount receivable,                        值計量，並使用反映於與有關客戶的個別融資
     discounted using the discount rate that would be reflected in a separate                  交易的折現率貼現，且利息收入根據實際利率
     financing transaction with the customer, and interest income is accrued                   法分開累計。倘合約載有向本集團提供重大融
     separately under the effective interest method. Where the contract contains               資利益的融資組成部份，則根據該合約確認的
     a financing component which provides a significant financing benefit to                   收入包括根據實際利率法合約責任附有的利息開
     the Group, revenue recognised under that contract includes the interest                   支。
     expense accreted on the contract liability under the effective interest
     method.

     The Group applies advantage of the practical expedient in paragraph 63                    本集團利用香港財務報告準則第15號第63段中
     of HKFRS 15 and does not adjust the consideration for any effects of a                    的實務簡便做法，並無就融資期為12個月或以
     significant financing component if the period of financing is 12 months or                下的重大融資組成部份的任何影響調整對價。
     less.

     (i)    Sales of properties                                                                (i)    物業銷售

            and obtain substantially all of the remaining benefits of the property.                   物業的收益。於收益確認日期前就出售的
            Deposits and instalments received on properties sold prior to the                         物業收取的訂金及分期款項計入財務狀況
            date of revenue recognition are included in the statement of financial                    表內合約負債項下。
            position under contract liabilities.

     (ii)   Sales of goods                                                                     (ii)   銷售貨品
            Revenue is recognised when the customer takes possession of and                           收益於客戶佔有並接納產品時確認。倘該
            accepts the products. If the products are a partial fulfilment of a                       等產品為部份履行涵蓋其他貨品及 ╱ 或服
            contract covering other goods and/or services, then the amount                            務的合約，則確認收益的金額為合約項下
            of revenue recognised is an appropriate proportion of the total                           總交易價的合適比例，按照相對獨立售價
            transaction price under the contract, allocated between all the goods                     基準根據合約協定的所有貨品及服務之間

 (b) Accounting policies of revenue recognition (continued)                              (b) 收益確認之會計政策（續）
     (iii) Construction income                                                               (iii) 建造收入
           A contract with a customer is classified by the Group as a                              當合約與受客戶管控的房地產資產工程相
           construction contract when the contract relates to work on real                         關時，本集團將與客戶所訂的合約列作建
           estate assets under the control of the customer and therefore the                       造合約，故本集團建造活動設置或提升受
           Group’s construction activities create or enhance an asset under the                    客戶管控的資產。
           customer’s control.

              When the outcome of a construction contract can be reasonably                         當建造合約的結果能合理計量時，合約收
              measured, revenue from the contract is recognised progressively over                  益採用成本法（即根據所產生的實際成本
              time using the cost-to-cost method, i.e. based on the proportion of                   佔估計總成本的比例）隨時間累計確認。
              the actual costs incurred relative to the estimated total costs.

              The likelihood of the Group earning contractual bonuses for early                     延遲完工而遭受合約罰款的可能性於作出
              completion or suffering contractual penalties for late completion are                 該等估計時考慮，因此，僅在已確認收
              taken into account in making these estimates, such that revenue                       益金額很可能不會大幅撥回時方會確認收
              is only recognised to the extent that it is highly probable that a                    益。
              significant reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue recognised
              will not occur.

              When the outcome of the contract cannot be reasonably measured,                       當合約的產出結果不能被合理計量時，收
              revenue is recognised only to the extent of contract costs incurred                   益僅在預期所產生的合約成本將予收回的
              that are expected to be recovered.                                                    情況下確認。

       (iv)   Rental income from leases                                                      (iv)   租賃的租金收入
              Rental income receivable under leases is recognised in profit or loss                 租賃應收租金收入在損益內按等額分期在
              in equal instalments over the periods covered by the lease term,                      租賃期限的期間內確認，惟當有替代基準
              except where an alternative basis is more representative of the                       更能代表使用租賃資產所產生的收益模式
              pattern of benefits to be derived from the use of the leased asset.                   時除外。所授租賃優惠作為應收租賃款項
              Lease incentives granted are recognised in profit or loss as an integral              淨額總額的組成部份在損益內確認。不依
              part of the aggregate net lease payments receivable. Variable lease                   賴於指數或利率之可變租賃付款按賺取租
              payments that do not depend on an index or a rate are recognised as                   金的會計期間的收入確認。
              income in the accounting period in which they are earned.

       (v)    Property management service income                                             (v)    物業管理費收入
              The Group provides property management services and bills the                         本集團提供物業管理服務，且業主須按月
              property owners a fixed lump sum amount for services provided on a                    就所提供的服務支付固定包乾費用。根據
              monthly basis. Under this arrangement, the Group acts as a principal                  該安排，本集團作為主事人主要負責向業
              and is primary responsible for providing the property management                      主提供物業管理服務。當本集團有權開具
              services to the property owners. The Group recognises the fee                         發票且直接與已完成履約的價值相關，
              received or receivable from property owners as its revenue when the                   以及全部相關物業管理成本為其服務成本
              Group has a right to invoice and that corresponds directly with the                   時，本集團將已收或應收業主的費用確認
              value of performance completed, and all related property management                   為其收益。
              costs as its cost of services.

 12 Income tax expenses (continued)                                                    12 所得稅開支（續）
 (a) Income tax expenses (continued)                                                   (a) 所得稅開支（續）
     LAT is levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                     土地增值稅就土地增值部份按30%至60%累進稅
     appreciation of land value, being the proceeds from sale of properties less           率徵收，土地增值部份即出售物業所得款項減
     deductible expenditures including amortisation of land use rights, borrowing          包括土地使用權攤銷、借貸成本及所有物業開
     costs and all property development expenditures.                                      發支出在內之可扣減開支。

       Dividend withholding tax                                                          股息預扣稅
       Pursuant to the Detailed Implementation Regulations for Implementation            根據《企業所得稅法實施細則》，中國公司從
       of the Corporate Income Tax Law, dividends distributed from the profits           2008年1月1日以後取得的利潤分配給境外投資
       generated by the PRC companies after 1 January 2008 to their foreign              者的股息，按10%的稅率預扣所得稅。倘中國
       investors shall be subject to a withholding income tax of 10%. A lower 5%         附屬公司的直接控股公司根據中國與香港之間的

       of the PRC subsidiaries are qualified as tax residents of Hong Kong               用較低的5%預扣稅率。
       according to the tax treat arrangements between the PRC and Hong Kong.

       During the financial year ended 31 December 2023 and 2022, deferred               截至2023年及2022年12月31日止財政年度，

16 Investment properties (continued)                                                    16 投資物業（續）
(a) Fair value hierarchy (continued)                                                    (a) 公允值等級架構（續）
    The fair value of the Group’s investment properties is determined using                 本集團之投資物業公允值採用收入資本化法及剩
    income capitalisation method and residual method.                                       餘法予以釐定。

     Income capitalisation method determines fair values by discounting the                   收入資本化法透過採用資本化率折現有關物業之
     projected cash flows associated with the properties using capitalisation                 預測現金流量釐定公允值。有關估值考慮到各
     rates. The valuation takes into account the expected market rental and                   物業之預期市場租金及空置率。所用貼現率乃
     vacancy rate of the respective properties. The discount rates used have                  就樓宇的質素及地點以及租戶信貸質素作出調
     been adjusted for the quality and location of the buildings and the tenant               整。公允值計量與預期市場租金正相關，惟與
     credit quality. The fair value measurement is positively correlated to the               空置率及資本化率負相關。
     expected market rental, and negatively correlated to the vacancy rate and
     the capitalisation rate.

     The residual method takes into account the residual value of the gross                   剩餘法考慮總發展價值並扣除尚未發生的建築成
     development value (“GDV”) after deduction of the outstanding construction                本、開支以及發展利潤後的剩餘價值。剩餘法
     costs and expenses as well as profit element. It involves firstly the                    首先計算假設建議發展項目於估值日已落成的合
     assessment of the aggregate GDV of the proposed development, as if                       計總發展價值。建議發展項目的估計成本包括
     completed, as at the valuation date. Estimated costs of the proposed                     建築成本、市場推廣支出、專業費用、財務費
     development include construction costs, marketing expense, professional                  用以及相關成本，另加開發商的風險及利潤撥
     fee, finance charges and associated costs, plus an allowance for the                     備。尚未發生的開發成本將由總發展價值中扣
     developer’s risk and profit. The outstanding development costs are then                  除，並得出標的物業的剩餘價值。
     deducted from the GDV and the resultant figure is the residual value of the
     subject property.

(b) Undiscounted lease payments under non-cancellable operating                         (b) 不可取消經營租賃之未貼現租賃付款
    leases
    The Group leases out its investment properties under operating leases. The                本集團根據經營租賃出租其投資物業。租約一
    leases typically run for an initial period of 1 to 20 years, with an option to            般初步為期1 至20 年，並有權於到期日續租，
    renew the leases after the date at which time all terms are renegotiated.                 同時重新商討所有條款。租賃付款一般每年增
    Lease payments are usually increased annually to reflect market rentals.                  加一次，以反映市場租金。本集團若干租約包
    Certain leases of the group contain variable lease payment terms that are                 含基於租戶收益的可變租賃付款條款。
    based on the revenue of tenants.

     Undiscounted lease payments under non-cancellable operating leases in                    本集團於未來期間應收於報告日期已訂立之不可
     place at the reporting date will be receivable by the Group in future periods            取消經營租賃之未貼現租賃付款如下：

 16 Investment properties (continued)                                                   16 投資物業（續）
 (c) Accounting policies of Investment properties                                       (c) 投資物業之會計政策
     Investment properties, principally freehold office buildings, are held for             投資物業主要為終身保有寫字樓，乃為長期租
     long-term rental yields and are not occupied by the Group. Investment                  金收益而持有，並非由本集團佔用。投資物業
     property is initially measured at cost, including related transaction costs            初始按成本計量，包括相關交易成本及借款成
     and where applicable borrowing costs. Subsequently, they are carried at                本（如適用）。其後按公允值計量。
     fair value.

       Fair value is based on active market prices, adjusted, if necessary, for any       公允值以活躍市場價格為基準，並在有需要的
       difference in the nature, location or condition of the specific asset. If the      情況下根據特定資產之性質、地點或條件之任
       information is not available, the Group uses alternative valuation methods         何差別調整公允值。倘無法取得此等資料，本
       such as recent prices on less active markets. Changes in fair values are           集團會採用其他估值方法，例如活躍度較低市
       presented in profit or loss as part of other income.                               場之近期價格。公允值變動於損益內列作其他
                                                                                          收入之一部份。

       If an item of owner-occupied property becomes an investment property               倘擁有人佔用物業項目因用途改變而成為投資物
       because its use has changed, any difference resulting between the carrying         業，則該項目於轉讓日期的賬面值與公允值之
       amount and the fair value of this item at the date of transfer is treated in       間產生的差額按與根據香港會計準則第16 號重
       the same way as a revaluation under HKAS 16. Any resulting increase in             估相同的方式處理。該物業產生的任何賬面值
       the carrying amount of the property is recognised in profit or loss to the         增長於損益中確認，惟須撥回之前減值虧損，
       extent that it reverses a previous impairment loss, with any remaining             任何剩餘增長於其他全面收益中確認，並直接
       increase recognised in other comprehensive income and increase directly            撥入權益中的重估盈餘。該物業產生的任何賬
       to equity in revaluation surplus within equity. Any resulting decrease in the      面值減少首先於其他全面收益中自先前確認的任
       carrying amount of the property is initially charged in other comprehensive        何重估盈餘中扣除，而任何剩餘減值於損益中
       income against any previously recognised revaluation surplus, with any             扣除。對於由持作待售之已落成物業或發展中
       remaining decrease charged to profit or loss. For a transfer from completed        物業轉為將按公允值列賬的投資物業，該日物
       properties held for sale or properties under development to investment             業公允值與其過往賬面值的任何差額於損益內確
       properties that will be carried at fair value, any difference between the fair     認。
       value of the property at that date and its previous carrying amount shall be
       recognised in profit or loss.

       The Group shall transfer a property from investment properties to properties       當一項物業啟動旨在出售的相關開發時，本集
       under development when it commences related development with a view to             團將投資物業轉為發展中物業。對於按公允值
       sale. For a transfer from investment properties that is carried at fair value      列賬的投資物業轉為發展中物業，相關發展物
       to properties under development, related properties under development              業須按轉撥日期的公允值確認。
       shall be recognised at fair value at the date of transfer.

       The Group classifies cash outflows to acquire or construct investment              本集團將收購或建造投資物業的現金流出分類為
       property as investing and rental inflows as operating cash flows.                  投資性現金流量，而租金流入則分類為經營現
                                                                                          金流量。

            – Unlisted equity investments in Mainland China                － 於中國大陸之非上市股權投資             99,315    80,604

 (i)   Accounting policies of financial assets                                          (i)   金融資產會計政策
       (a) Classification                                                                     (a) 分類
           The Group classifies its financial assets in the following measurement                 本集團將其金融資產按以下計量類別分
           categories:                                                                            類：

              •     those to be measured subsequently at fair value (either through             •   其後按公允值計量（計入其他全面收
                    other comprehensive income (“OCI”), or through profit or loss),                 益（「其他全面收益」）或計入損益）
                    and                                                                             的金融資產；及

              •     those to be measured at amortised cost.                                     •   將按攤銷成本計量的金融資產。

              The classification depends on the entity’s business model for                     該分類取決於實體管理金融資產及合約現
              managing the financial assets and the contractual terms of the cash               金流量年期之業務模式。
              flows.

              For assets measured at fair value, gains and losses will either be                就按公允值計量之資產而言，收益及虧損
              recorded in profit or loss or OCI. For investments in debt instruments,           將於損益或其他全面收益入賬。就於債務
              this will depend on the business model in which the investment                    工具的投資而言，將視乎持有投資的業務
              is held. For investments in equity instruments, this will depend on               模式而定。對於權益工具投資，這將取決
              whether the Group has made an irrevocable election at the time of                 於本集團在初始確認時是否作出不可撤銷
              initial recognition to account for the equity investment at FVOCI.                的選擇而將其作為透過其他全面收益按公
                                                                                                允值計算之股權投資入賬。

              The Group reclassifies debt investments when and only when its                    僅當管理該等資產之業務模式發生變動
              business model for managing those assets changes.                                 時，本集團才會對債務投資進行重新分

                                                                                                       支。

            Financial assets with embedded derivatives are considered in their                         在確定具有嵌入衍生工具的金融資產的現

            payment of principal and interest.                                                         融資產的整體進行考慮。

            into which the Group classifies its debt instruments:                                      類別：

            •     Amortised cost: Assets that are held for collection of contractual                   •     按攤銷成本：持作收回合約現金流
                  cash flows where those cash flows represent solely payments of                             量之資產，倘該等現金流量僅為支

                  income from these financial assets is included in finance income                           量。該等金融資產之利息收入乃按

       shall be valid and effective for a period of 10 year from that date, after                  有效。有效期屆滿後，則不會再授出任何購股
       which period no further options will be issued but in all other respects the                權，但在所有其他方面，計劃條文仍具十足效

    hand-over to the customers.

    The Group may require the customers to pay off the remaining balance of                   本集團可要求客戶於建造工程仍在進行之協定時
    the consideration within an agreed timeframe while constructions are still                間內而非於有關物業落成時償還剩餘代價。有
    ongoing, rather than on the completion of the relevant properties. Such                   關提前付款計劃會導致於整個剩餘物業建造期間
    advance payment schemes result in contract liabilities being recognised                   按全額合約價格確認合約負債。

    the contract price.

    Sichuan Haofu Industrial Co., Ltd. (the “Litigation”) regarding the disputes            公司就成都市成華區「198」叢樹片區投資的收

    handle the Litigation, and will rigorously defend the Litigation to protect             不會對本集團的營運造成重大不利財務影響。

45 Summary of other potentially material accounting policies                            45 其他潛在重大會計政策概要
45.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                  45.1 合併及權益會計原則
     (a) Subsidiaries                                                                        (a) 附屬公司
          Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which               附屬公司均為本集團擁有控制權的所有實
          the Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group                    體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團承擔或
          is exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement                 擁有自其參與該實體產生之可變回報之
          with the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through                    權利及有能力透過行使權力以影響其回報
          its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully               時，本集團即控制該實體。附屬公司自控
          consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the                      制權轉移至本集團之日起全面合併。附屬
          Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                      公司自控制停止之日起解除合併。

           The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                      本集團使用收購會計法對業務合併進行會
           combinations by the group (refer to note 45.2).                                           計處理（參閱附註45.2）。

           Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on                              公司之間之交易、集團公司間交易之結餘
           transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised                           及未變現收益予以對銷。未變現虧損亦予
           losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence                       以對銷，除非交易有證據證明已轉讓資產
           of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                            出現減值。附屬公司會計政策已按需要作
           of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                               出變動，以確保與本集團所採納之政策貫
           consistency with the policies adopted by the group.                                       徹一致。

           Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries                       附屬公司業績及權益中的非控股權益分別
           are shown separately in the consolidated statement of profit or loss,                     於綜合損益表、全面收益表、權益變動表
           statement of comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity                         及財務狀況表中單獨呈列。
           and statement of financial position respectively.

     (b)   Associates                                                                         (b)    聯營公司
           Associates are all entities over which the group has significant                          聯營公司指所有本集團對其擁有重大影響
           influence but not control or joint control. This is generally the case                    力但無控制權或聯合控制權的實體。一般
           where the group holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                           情況下，本集團持有20%至50%投票權。
           Investments in associates are accounted for using the equity method                       於聯營公司之投資於初步按成本確認後採
           of accounting, after initially being recognised at cost.                                  用權益會計法入賬。

        Annual Report 年報 2023

      (c) Joint arrangements                                                                  (c) 合營安排
           Under HKFRS 11 Joint Arrangements investments in joint                                 根據香港財務報告準則第11號聯合安排，
           arrangements are classified as either joint operations or joint                        於聯合安排的投資被分類為聯合經營或合
           ventures. The classification depends on the contractual rights and                     營公司。該分類取決於各投資者的合約性
           obligations of each investor, rather than the legal structure of the                   權利及責任，而非聯合安排的法律架構。
           joint arrangement. The Group has assessed the nature of its joint                      本集團已評估其聯合安排的性質，將其釐

              Interests in joint ventures are accounted for using the equity method,             於合營公司的投資於綜合財務狀況表於初
              after initially being recognised at cost in the consolidated statement             步按成本確認後以會計權益法入賬。
              of financial position.

       (d)   Equity method                                                                 (d)   權益法
             Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially                根據權益會計法，投資初始按成本確認，
             recognised at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                 其後進行調整以於損益內確認本集團分佔

             or loss, and the Group’s share of movements in other comprehensive                  合收益確認其分佔被收購方其他綜合收益
             income of the investee in other comprehensive income. Dividends                     的變動。已收或應收聯營公司及合營公司
             received or receivable from associates and joint ventures are                       的股息確認為投資賬面值扣減。
             recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

              Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment                倘本集團應佔權益入賬投資的虧損等於或
              equals or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other                  超過其於該實體的權益（包括任何其他無
              unsecured long-term receivables, the Group does not recognize                      抵押長期應收款項），則本集團不會確認
              further losses, unless it has incurred obligations or made payments                進一步虧損，除非已代表另一實體承擔責
              on behalf of the other entity.                                                     任或作出付款。

              Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group and its                         本集團與其聯營公司及合營公司之間交易
              associates and joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of the                  的未變現收益按本集團於該等實體的權益
              Group’s interest in these entities. Unrealised losses are also                     予以對銷。未變現虧損亦予以對銷，但如
              eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment               有關交易提供所轉讓資產減值之證據則除
              of the asset transferred. Accounting policies of equity-accounted                  外。權益入賬被投資方的會計政策已在需

 45.2 Business combinations                                                                45.2 業務合併
      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                  本集團採用收購會計法就所有業務合併入賬，
      combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are                而無論有否收購股權投資或其他資產。收購一
      acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary               間附屬公司的轉讓代價包括：
      comprises the:

       •      fair values of the assets transferred                                          •   為所轉讓資產公允值

       •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business             •   對被收購業務前擁有人所產生的負債

       •      equity interests issued by the group                                           •   本集團所發行股權

       •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent               •   或有代價安排產生的任何資產或負債的公
              consideration arrangement, and                                                     允值，及

       •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.              •   附屬公司任何先前存在股權的公允值。

       Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed       於業務合併時所收購的可識別資產及所承擔的
       in a business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially            負債及或有負債，初始按收購當日的公允值計
       at their fair values at the acquisition date. The group recognises any non-           量，惟少數例外情況除外。本集團根據個別收
       controlling interest in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition          購交易按公允值或非控股權益應佔被收購實體可
       basis either at fair value or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate         識別資產淨值的比例確認於被收購實體的任何非
       share of the acquired entity’s net identifiable assets.                               控股權益。

       Concentration test as prescribed by HKFRS 3 “Business Combination”                    本集團已應用香港財務報告準則第3號「業務合
       has been applied by the Group to consider whether the set of activities               併」所規定的集中度測試，以考慮所收購附屬公
       and assets of subsidiaries acquired are individually businesses. If the fair          司的一系列活動及資產是否為個別業務。倘所
       value of total net assets acquired substantially equals to the fair value of          收購資產淨值總額的公允值與已收購可識別資產
       an identifiable asset acquired, the transaction would be accounted for as             的公允值大致相若，則交易將作為資產收購而
       asset acquisition instead of business acquisition. The consideration is then          非業務收購入賬。屆時會將代價直接分配至收
       directly allocated to the individual identifiable assets acquired and liabilities     購的個別可識別資產及承擔的負債。
       assumed.

       Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                   收購相關成本於產生時支銷。

           or loss and translation differences on non-monetary assets such as                      分類於其他綜合收益之權益）的換算差額

           are recognised in other comprehensive income.

45.6 Property, plant and equipment                                                        45.6 物業、廠房及設備
     Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less depreciation             物業、廠房及設備均以歷史成本扣除折舊及任
     and any impairment losses. Historical cost includes expenditure that is                   何減值虧損列賬。歷史成本包括購買該等項目
     directly attributable to the acquisition of the items.                                    直接應佔的開支。

     Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount or recognised                 後續成本只有在很可能為本集團帶來與該項目有
     as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when it is probable that future                  關的未來經濟利益，而該項目的成本能可靠計
     economic benefits associated with the item will flow to the Group and                      量時，才包括在資產的賬面值或確認為一項單
     the cost of the item can be measured reliably. The carrying amount of                      獨資產（如適用）。已更換部份的賬面值已被終
     the replaced part is derecognised. All other repairs and maintenance are                   止確認。所有其他維修及保養開支乃於其產生
     charged to profit or loss during the financial period in which they are                    之財政期間內自損益扣除。
     incurred.

           Any impairment is recognised immediately as an expense and is not                           本之較高者。任何減值即時確認為開支，

 45.8 Leases                                                                           45.8 租賃
      Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability           租賃於本集團可使用租賃資產當日確認為使用權
      at the date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.              資產及相應負債。

       The Group leases various properties. Lease terms are negotiated on an             本集團租賃多間物業，並逐個釐定租賃條款，
       individual basis and contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.      當中載列各種不同條款及條件。該等物業租賃
       These property lease agreements do not impose any covenants, but leased           協議不施加任何契諾，但租賃資產不得用作借
       properties may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                    款抵押物。

       Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a           租賃產生的資產及負債初始按現值計量。租賃
       present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the       負債包括以下租賃付款之淨現值：
       following lease payments:

       •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments), less any           •     固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減任何應收
              lease incentives receivable;                                                     租賃優惠；

       •      variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially     •     基於指數或利率計量之可變租賃付款，採
              measured using the index or rate as at the commencement date;                    用於開始日期的指數或利率初始計量；

       •      amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value           •     本集團預期應支付的剩餘價值擔保金額；
              guarantees;

       •      the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is reasonably         •     購買權之行使價格（倘本集團合理地確定
              certain to exercise that option; and                                             行使該選擇權）；及

       •      payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term         •     支付終止租賃的罰款（倘租期反映本集團
              reflects the Group exercising that option.                                       行使權利終止租約）。

45.8 Leases (continued)                                                                  45.8 租賃（續）
     Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension options are                 根據合理確定延期選擇權支付之租賃付款亦計入
     also included in the measurement of the liability.                                       負債計量內。

     The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the                 租賃付款採用租賃所隱含之利率予以貼現。倘
     lease. If that rate cannot be readily determined, the lessee’s incremental                無法輕易釐定該利率，則使用承租人的增量借
     borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the individual lessee would have              款利率，即個別承租人在類似條款及條件的類
     to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in               似經濟環境中借入獲得類似價值資產所需資金所
     a similar economic environment with similar terms and conditions.                         必須支付的利率。

     To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                                   為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：

     •    where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by the                    •      在可能情況下，使用個別承租人最近獲得
          individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect changes in                       的第三方融資為出發點作出調整，以反映
          financing conditions since third party financing was received;                              自獲得第三方融資以來融資狀況之變動；

     •    uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free interest rate                  •     使用累加法，首先就本集團所持有租賃
          adjusted for credit risk for leases held by the Group, which does not                      （最近並無第三方融資者）的信貸風險調整
          have recent third party financing; and                                                     無風險利率；及

     •    makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term, country, currency                •      對租賃作出特定調整，例如期限、國家、
          and security.                                                                               貨幣及抵押。

     If a readily observable amortising loan rate is available to the individual               倘個別承租人可獲得與租賃付款情況類似且隨時
     lessee (through recent financing or market data) which has a similar                      可觀察之攤銷貸款利率（透過近期融資或市場數
     payment profile to the lease, then the Group entities use that rate as a                  據），則集團內實體將該利率作為釐定增量借款
     starting point to determine the incremental borrowing rate.                               利率之出發點。

     Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance cost. The                      租賃付款於本金及財務成本之間分配。財務成
     finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to                  本於租賃期內自損益中扣除，以便各期間的負
     produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of the              債餘額產生持續的定期利率。
     liability for each period.

     Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the following:                        使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下各項：

     •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease liabilities;                          •      租賃負債的初始計量金額；

     •    any lease payments made at or before the commencement date less                      •      於開始日期或之前所作的任何租賃付款，
          any lease incentives received;                                                              減去所得的任何租賃獎勵；

     •    any initial direct costs; and                                                        •      任何初始直接成本；及

     •    restoration costs.                                                                   •      修復成本。

      Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the shorter of the                  使用權資產一般按資產可使用年期或租賃期（以

      Goodwill and intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life or                  具無限使用年期之商譽及無形資產或未能即時使
      intangible assets not ready to use are not subject to amortisation and are             用之無形資產毋須攤銷，並會每年進行減值測
      tested annually for impairment. Assets that are subject to amortisation are            試。當有事件出現或情況改變顯示賬面值可能
      reviewed for impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances                    無法收回時，會審閱須作攤銷的資產是否已經
      indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An impairment                減值。減值虧損按資產的賬面值超出其可收回
      loss is recognised for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount                 金額的差額確認。可收回金額為資產的公允值
      exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of                減出售成本及使用價值兩者中的較高者。於評
      an asset’s fair value less costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes            估減值時，資產按獨立可識別現金流量（現金產
      of assessing impairment, assets are Grouped at the lowest levels for                   生單位）之最低層次組合進行分組。除商譽外已
      which there are separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units).            蒙受減值的非金融資產於各報告期末均就減值是
      Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are               否可以撥回進行檢討。
      reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at each of each reporting

45.10 Impairment of financial assets                                                       45.10 金融資產減值
      The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses (“ECL”) on                本集團就根據香港財務報告準則第9號須予減值
      financial assets which are subject to impairment under HKFRS 9 (including                  的金融資產（包括貿易及其他應收款、給予合營
      trade and other receivables, loans to joint ventures and associates, cash                  公司及聯營公司之貸款、現金及現金等價物、
      and cash equivalents, restricted cash and bank deposits and financial                      受限制現金及銀行存款以及財務擔保）的預期信
      guarantee).                                                                                用損失（「預期信用損失」）確認損失撥備。

      The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect changes in                  預期信用損失金額於各報告日更新，反映自初
      credit risk since initial recognition. Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that                始確認起的信用風險變動。存續期預期信用損
      will result from all possible default events over the expected life of the                 失指相關工具的預期使用期內所有可能的違約事
      relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the                  件產生之預期信用損失。相反，12個月預期信
      portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events that                用損失（「12 個月預期信用損失」）將預期於報
      are possible within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment are                     告日期後12 個月內可能發生的違約事件預期導
      done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for                  致之存續期預期信用損失部份。評估乃根據本
      factors that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an               集團的歷史信用損失經驗進行，並根據債務人
      assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting date as well as                 特有的因素、一般經濟狀況以及對報告日期當
      the forecast of future conditions.                                                         前狀況的評估以及對未來狀況的預測作出調整。

      The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade receivables. To                         本集團始終就應收賬款確認整個存續期預期信用
      measure ECL, trade receivables have been grouped based on shared credit                    損失。為計量預期信用損失，應收賬款已按共
      risk characteristics and the days past due. The ECL on these assets are                    有之信貸風險特徵及逾期天數分組。該等資產
      assessed using simplified expected credit loss model.                                      之預期信用損失乃使用簡化預期信用損失模式評
                                                                                                 估。

      For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss allowance equal to                  對於所有其他工具，本集團計量的損失撥備相
      12m ECL, unless when there has been a significant increase in credit risk                  當於12個月預期信用損失，除非自初始確認起
      since initial recognition, the Group recognizes lifetime ECL. The assessment               信用風險出現顯著增長，則本集團確認存續期
      of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on significant                       間預期信用損失。是否應確認存續期預期信用
      increases in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial                   損失的評估乃基於自初始確認以來發生違約之可
      recognition.                                                                               能性或風險的顯著增加。

45.11 Offsetting financial instruments                                                     45.11 抵銷金融工具
      Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount reported in                 若具法律強制執行權利以抵銷已確認款項及有
      the consolidated statement of financial position when there is a legally                   意按淨額基準結算，或同時變現資產及清償負
      enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts and there is an                         債，則金融資產及負債可予以抵銷，而有關淨
      intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability           額則於綜合財務狀況表內呈報。法定可執行權
      simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not be contingent on                    利必須不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一般業務
      future events and must be enforceable in the normal course of business                     過程中以及倘本公司或對手方一旦出現違約、
      and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the company or the                無償債能力或破產時，這也必須具有約束力。
      counterparty.

 45.12 Inventories                                                                     45.12 存貨
       Inventories are stated at the lower of cost or net realisable value. Cost             存貨按成本或可變現淨值的較低者列賬。成本
       is determined using the weighted average method. Net realisable value                 採用加權平均法釐定。可變現淨值乃於日常業
       is the estimated selling price in the ordinary course of business, less the           務過程中之估計售價減銷售所需的估計成本。
       estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

 45.13 Trade and other receivables                                                     45.13 貿易及其他應收款
       Trade receivables are amounts due from buyers for properties sold or                  應收賬款乃於日常業務過程中就銷售物業或提供
       services performed in the ordinary course of business. If collection of trade         服務而應收買方之款項。倘貿易及其他應收款
       and other receivables is expected in one year or less (or in the normal               預期於一年或更短時間內（或於正常業務營運週
       operating cycle of the business if longer), they are classified as current            期（如較長））收回，則會被分類為流動資產。
       assets. If not, they are presented as non-current assets.                             否則，在非流動資產中列報。

       Trade and other receivables are recognised initially at fair value and            應收賬款及其他應收賬款初始按公允值確認，
       subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective interest              其後則使用實際利率法按攤銷成本，減去減值
       method, less provision for impairment.                                            撥備計量。

 45.14 Contract assets and contract liabilities                                        45.14 合約資產及合約負債
       Upon entering into a contract with a buyer, the Group obtains rights to               在與買家簽訂合約時，本集團有權獲得來自買
       receive consideration from the buyer and assumes performance obligations              家支付的合約價款，與此同時承擔將貨物或服
       to transfer goods or provide services to the buyer. The combination of                務轉移給買家的履約義務。這些權利和履行義
       those rights and performance obligations gives rise to a net asset or a               務的結合導致了淨資產或淨負債的產生，這取
       net liability depending on the relationship between the remaining rights              決於剩餘權利和履行義務之間的關係。合約計
       and the performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised              量的剩餘收款權超過了剩餘履約義務的部份確認
       as contract assets if the measure of the remaining rights exceeds the                 為合約資產。反之，剩餘履約義務超過剩餘收
       measure of the remaining performance obligations. Conversely, the contract            款權的部份確認為合約負債。
       is a liability and recognised as contract liabilities if the measure of the
       remaining performance obligations exceeds the measure of the remaining
       rights.

       The Group recognises the incremental costs of obtaining a contract with           本集團為獲得與買家簽訂的合約而產生的增量成
       a buyer as contract acquisition cost within contract assets if the Group          本預計可收回時，則將其確認為合約資產中的
       expects to recover those costs.                                                   合約取得成本。

       institutions, other short-term, highly liquid investments with original               其他短期高流通投資（原本到期日不超過三個
       maturities of three months or less that are readily convertible to known              月，並可即時轉換為已知數額的現金，惟須受
       amounts of cash and which are subject to an insignificant risk of changes             非重大價值變動風險所限）及銀行透支。於綜合
       in value, and bank overdrafts. Bank overdrafts are shown within borrowings            財務狀況表中，銀行透支列入流動負債的借款

 45.19 Borrowings                                                                          45.19 借貸
       Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction costs               借貸初始按公允值扣除所產生的交易成本確
       incurred. Borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost;                         認。借貸其後按攤銷成本計量，如扣除交易成
       any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the                    本之後的所得收益與贖回價值出現差額，則於
       redemption value is recognised in profit or loss over the period of the                   借貸期內以實際利率法在損益表中確認。
       borrowings using the effective interest method.

       Borrowings are removed from the consolidated statement financial position                當合約中規定之責任解除、取消或屆滿時，借
       when the obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled                   貸自綜合財務狀況表中剔除。已銷除或轉讓予
       or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a financial                    另一方之金融負債之賬面值與已支付代價（包括
       liability that has been extinguished or transferred to another party and the             任何已轉讓之非現金資產或所承擔之負債）之間
       consideration paid, including any non-cash assets transferred or liabilities             之差額，在損益中確認為財務成本。
       assumed, is recognised in profit or loss as finance costs.

       Where the terms of a financial liability are renegotiated and the entity                 倘重新議定一項金融負債的條款，而實體向債
       issues equity instruments to a creditor to extinguish all or part of the                 權人發行股本工具以消除所有或部份負債（債務
       liability (debt for equity swap), a gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss,        或權益互換），則相關盈虧於損益內確認，並按
       which is measured as the difference between the carrying amount of the                   該金融負債的賬面值與發行的權益工具的公允值
       financial liability and the fair value of the equity instruments issued.                 之差額計量。

       Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group has an                 除非本集團有權無條件將債務清償遞延至報告期
       unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12 months          後至少12個月，否則借貸將被分類為流動負債。
       after the reporting period.

 45.20 Borrowing costs                                                                     45.20 借貸成本
       General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,            直接歸屬於收購、興建或生產合資格資產（須經

       necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready for their intended             般及特定借款成本會加入該等資產的成本中，
       use or sale, are added to the cost of those assets, until such time as the                直至資產大致上備妥可作擬定用途或銷售為止。
       assets are substantially ready for their intended use or sale.

       Investment income earned on the temporary investment of specific                         在特定借款撥作合資格資產的支出前暫時用作投
       borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from               資所賺取的投資收入會自可撥充資本的借款成本
       the borrowing costs eligible for capitalisation.                                         中扣除。

       All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the period in              所有其他借貸成本於其產生期間在損益確認。
       which they are incurred.

45.21 Current and deferred income tax                                                     45.21 當期及遞延所得稅
      The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on the                 期內所得稅費用或抵免為當期應課稅收入按各司
      current period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for               法權區適用所得稅率之應付稅項，經暫時差異
      each jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities              及未使用稅項虧損產生的遞延稅項資產及負債變
      attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                           動調整。

     (a)   Current income tax                                                                   (a)    即期所得稅
           The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax                         即期所得稅開支乃根據本公司及其附屬公
           laws enacted or substantively enacted at the end of the reporting                           司和聯營公司業務所在並產生應課稅收入
           period in the countries where the company and its subsidiaries                              之國家於各報告期末已頒佈或實質已頒佈
           and associates operate and generate taxable income. Management                              之稅法計算。管理層就適用稅務法規詮釋
           periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to                       所規限的情況定期評估報稅表的狀況，並
           situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation                  權衡稅務機關是否可能接受不確定稅務處
           and considers whether it is probable that a taxation authority will                         理。本集團基於最大可能金額或預期價值
           accept an uncertain tax treatment. The Group measures its tax                               計量稅項結餘，取決於哪種方法提供不確
           balances either based on the most likely amount or the expected                             定性解決方法的更佳預測。
           value, depending on which method provides a better prediction of the
           resolution of the uncertainty.

     (b)   Deferred income tax                                                                  (b)   遞延所得稅
           Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability method, on                    就資產和負債的稅基與資產和負債在綜合
           temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets and                          財務報表的賬面值的差額而產生的暫時性
           liabilities and their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial                       差異利用負債法撥備遞延所得稅。然而，
           statements. However, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if they                   倘遞延稅項負債來自對商譽的初始確認，
           arise from the initial recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is                     則不作記賬。倘遞延所得稅來自交易（不

           temporary differences. Deferred income tax is determined using tax                         稅以報告期末前頒佈或實質上頒佈的稅率

           the end of the reporting period and are expected to apply when the                         關遞延所得稅資產變現或遞延所得稅負債
           related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred income                       償付時應用。

 45.24 Provisions (continued)                                                            45.24 撥備（續）
       Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an                 倘出現多項類似責任，將會考慮整體責任所屬
       outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the class           類別以釐定清償責任會否導致資源流出。即使
       of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood             同一類別責任中任何一項可能流出資源的機會不
       of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the same class of                大，仍會確認撥備。
       obligations may be small.

       Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures expected           撥備採用反映當時市場對金錢時間值及有關責任
       to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects          特定風險之評估之稅前比率，按照預期履行有
       current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                 關責任所需支出之現值計量。時間流逝引起之
       specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to passage of         撥備增加確認為利息開支。
       time is recognised as interest expense.

 45.25 Interest income                                                                   45.25 利息收入
       Interest income is calculated by applying the effective interest rate to the            金融資產之利息收入按實際利率法乘以金融資產
       gross carrying amount of a financial asset except for financial assets that             賬面總額計算，惟後續會發生信用減值之金融
       subsequently become credit-impaired. For credit-impaired financial assets               資產除外。就信貸減值的金融資產而言，其利
       the effective interest rate is applied to the net carrying amount of the                息收入是用實際利率乘以金融資產賬面淨額（經
       financial asset (after deduction of the loss allowance).                                扣除損失撥備）得出。

 45.28 Financial guarantee contracts                                                     45.28 財務擔保合約
       Financial guarantee contracts are recognised in respect of the financial                財務擔保合約乃就本集團為物業購買者向銀行提
       guarantee provided by the Group to the banks for property purchasers.                   供財務擔保確認。

       Financial guarantee liabilities are recognised initially at fair value plus         財務擔保負債初始按公允值加財務擔保負債直接
       transaction costs that are directly attributable to the issue of the financial      產生之交易成本確認。初始確認後，有關負債
       guarantee liabilities. After initial recognition, such liabilities are measured     按履行現行責任所需開支之最佳估計現值與初始
       at the higher of the present value of the best estimate of the expenditure          確認金額減已確認費用累計攤銷之較高者計量。
       required to settle the present obligation and the amount initially recognised
       less cumulative amortisation of fees recognised.

       Financial guarantee liabilities are derecognised from the consolidated              當且僅當合約中規定之責任解除、取消或屆滿
       statement of financial position when, and only when, the obligation                 時，財務擔保負債自綜合財務狀況表中終止確
       specified in the contract is discharged or cancelled or expired.                    認。
